# E1-T14 · CI publishes the api Docker image to GHCR + consumer compose file

## Objective
The web executor (and later staging, E0-T13) must run the **real core** without building Java: the web sandbox cannot write into the api repo, and staging should deploy an image, not a checkout. Make CI build the existing `Dockerfile` on every green `main` push and publish it to GitHub Container Registry, and provide a compose file that starts core + Mongo replica set + the fictional seeds from that image.

## Spec references
E0-T03 (CI), E0-T13 (`DEPLOY.md`, staging), E1-T10 (`bin/e1-smoke`, seeds), `docs/DEPLOY.md` (Caddy recipe from E1-T13).

## Context to load
`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `Dockerfile`, `docker-compose.yml`, `bin/e1-smoke`, `seeds/*.yaml`, `docs/DEPLOY.md`, this file.

## Fixed conventions
- Image: `ghcr.io/jboixtcm/agilityhub-core-api` with tags `main` (latest green main) and `sha-<short>`; built with the multi-stage `Dockerfile` (JRE 21 runtime, non-root user, `HEALTHCHECK` on `/api/v1/health`), for `linux/amd64` **and** `linux/arm64` (Jordi's Mac is Apple silicon; the droplet is amd64). Publish job runs only after the test job succeeds and only on `main` (not on `[skip ci]` commits — the existing behaviour). Auth: `GITHUB_TOKEN` with `packages: write`; the package visibility follows the repo (private) — document `docker login ghcr.io` with a `gh auth token` for local pulls.
- `docker-compose.consumer.yml` (new, committed): services `mongo` (replica set, same init as today), `core` (the GHCR image, `SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=local`, `MAIL_LOCAL_DIRECTORY` mounted, `OIDC_MASTER_KEY` ephemeral in local, ports `8080`), and a one-shot `seed` service that runs `club:apply seeds/club-canic.yaml` + `seeds/club-minim.yaml` with `SEED_PASSWORD` from the environment (`.env.consumer.example`). No real secret in the repo. Hosts: `app.example.test`/`admin.example.test`/`id.example.test` resolved by the consumer through `/etc/hosts` or the Vite proxy `Host` header (document both).
- `bin/e1-smoke` gains `--image` mode (or an env var) so it can run against the compose stack instead of a locally built jar — the same assertions, proving the published image works.
- `docs/DEPLOY.md`: section «Run the published image» (compose commands, login, seeds, ports) and the staging note (E0-T13 will pull `sha-…` tags).

## Steps
1. Workflow job `publish-image` (needs: tests) with `docker/setup-qemu-action`, `docker/setup-buildx-action`, `docker/login-action` (ghcr), `docker/metadata-action` (tags `main`, `sha-…`), `docker/build-push-action` (cache to GHA).
2. `docker-compose.consumer.yml` + `.env.consumer.example` + `bin/consumer-up` / `bin/consumer-down` helpers (thin wrappers).
3. `bin/e1-smoke --image ghcr.io/…:main` path; run it locally against the compose stack once the first image exists (if the image is not yet published when you run, build it locally with the same Dockerfile and tag it identically — say so in the report).
4. `docs/DEPLOY.md` section; `README.md` pointer.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `./mvnw -q verify` → green (nothing functional changes; the workflow file is validated with `actionlint` if available, else by a dry read).
- `docker build -t ghcr.io/jboixtcm/agilityhub-core-api:local .` → success; `docker compose -f docker-compose.consumer.yml up -d --wait` with that tag → `curl http://localhost:8080/api/v1/health` `UP`; `bin/e1-smoke --image …:local` → all PASS; `docker compose … down -v`.
- Paste the workflow diff.

## Definition of Done
Green `main` pushes publish `:main` + `:sha-…` images (first real run verified by the organizer in the Actions log); consumer compose + seeds + smoke work against the image; DEPLOY documented.

## Evidence to return
Outputs above.

---

## Executor report
Completed E1-T14 locally on 2026-09-09. Full verification passes: 244 unit/contract tests and 357 integration tests, no failures/errors/skips; Maven coverage and architecture gates pass. Image and jar E1 smoke modes pass. The organizer owns the first real GHCR publication check after the session is published.

### Branch / commits
Working tree only. No git write command, branch, commit or push was run. The session publish script owns publication on `main`.

### Evidence (full command outputs)
All captured stdout/stderr is included below, including failed attempts. Hashes/tokens are truncated under AGENTS.md rule 6; otherwise outputs are not summarized or shortened. Empty output is identified explicitly. Temporary Maven/Buildx caches are writable sandbox paths. The temporary consumer env file contains a generated password (never pasted), `CORE_IMAGE=ghcr.io/jboixtcm/agilityhub-core-api:local`, `COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=e1-t14-verification` and `CONSUMER_LOG_LEVEL=ERROR`.

Maven attempt 1 lost fixed-January SecurityEvent fixtures to Mongo's real-time TTL worker. Attempt 2's temporary per-test wall-clock workaround exposed Mongo millisecond precision and the same TTL race in IdentityCurlIT. The final change disables only the disposable integration server's TTL worker, retains the fixed MockClock, TTL index assertions and application-expiry tests, and leaves SecurityEventIT unchanged. Production and both smoke stacks keep normal TTL behavior. The runtime-check retry corrects an ad-hoc probe's expected output text; both seeds already reported zero changes.

The plain `curl -fsS http://localhost:8080/api/v1/health` probe returned exit 22 with full stderr `curl: (22) The requested URL returned error: 500`. Explicit IPv4, matching Compose's 127.0.0.1 binding, returned UP below. No pre-existing host listener was changed.

#### Maven, attempt 1

Command: `MAVEN_USER_HOME=/tmp/e1-t09-maven MAVEN_OPTS=-Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/e1-t09-maven/repository ./mvnw -q verify`

### Files changed
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: `tests` job id (existing check name retained), cancel superseded runs, publish after tests and secrets on main pushes only, pinned Docker actions, GITHUB_TOKEN package-write scope, metadata main/short-sha tags, both architectures and GHA build cache.
- `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`: native build stage for the portable Java jar, packaged local seeds, private non-root mailbox directory; existing JRE 21 and image healthcheck retained.
- `docker-compose.consumer.yml`, `.env.consumer.example`, `.gitignore`, `bin/consumer-up`, `bin/consumer-down`: standalone image consumer, Mongo replica-set readiness, required environment password, successful-seed startup gate, local mail volume, loopback port, helper commands and committed blank example.
- `seeds/club-canic-consumer.yaml`: fictional canonical Cànic dataset with only app/admin hosts changed to example.test; installed in the image at seeds/club-canic.yaml. Original seeds unchanged.
- `bin/e1-smoke`: optional image mode runs the same assertions and CLI from an isolated consumer project; mounts only the fictional import CSV, reads/deletes local mailbox messages, preserves Docker connection/auth discovery, and cleans up on success/failure. Existing jar mode passes too.
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/support/AbstractIntegrationTest.java`: disable Mongo's wall-clock TTL worker only in the disposable singleton used by fixed-clock integration fixtures.
- `docs/DEPLOY.md`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`: image login/start/update/cleanup, local hosts/proxy/mailbox, seed behavior, image smoke and staging handoff.
- `roadmap/tasks/E1-T14.md`, `roadmap/MESSAGES.md`: this report and organizer handoff; STATUS regenerated through check.py.

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- E1-T14 is infrastructure work; it introduces no business R-xx-nn rule, endpoint or API contract change. No OpenAPI snapshot change is needed.
- Existing E1 assertions run in both modes: magic-link login, tenant/member /me, remembered profile, HttpOnly host-only cookie rotation/reuse and family revocation, first password, seven locales, five-failure lockout, admin impersonation and handoff, required S256 PKCE, code replay, claims/userinfo, session revoke and a 50-row Learn dry-run with unchanged identity/audit/outbox data.
- Existing T-01-04/T-01-08 IdentityCurlIT and T-01-15 SecurityEventIT regressions pass with the corrected disposable Mongo clock boundary. All 601 existing tests and required Maven gates pass.
- Operational evidence verifies image UID 10001, mailbox mode 0700, Mongo PRIMARY, private Mongo port, API loopback binding, no seed password in the API process, zero-change reapplication, required-password guard and complete project cleanup. actionlint v1.7.12 passes; downloaded official release checksum was verified.

### Assumptions
- Dependencies E0-T03 and E1-T10 are verified. No awaiting-verification dependency was used.
- The task's docker-compose.yml reference means the existing compose.yaml; the consumer embeds equivalent Mongo initialization so it can be copied into the web workspace without checkout mounts.
- Local verification built the requested :local tag using the same Dockerfile on arm64. No remote push/pull was attempted. The organizer must verify the first main Actions publication, private package access and amd64/arm64 manifests after the session publish script runs.
- The canonical Cànic seed contains product domains. A separate local seed variant preserves its dataset and supplies app.example.test/admin.example.test; id.example.test is global issuer configuration. Static parity and image smoke were checked. Future seed edits should keep the local variant aligned.
- Image smoke owns a fresh random Compose project and port, so it does not mutate a consumer's existing accounts. CLI processes and API use local ephemeral signing; staging must supply its persistent key and deployment configuration in E0-T13.
- The integration-only TTL setting is a Mongo server option, not an application/catalog parameter. TTL indexes and application expiry stay tested; asynchronous real-time TTL deletion is not exercised by the fixed-clock suite. Local image/jar smoke uses ordinary Mongo TTL behavior. Mongo documents the option at https://www.mongodb.com/docs/manual/reference/parameters/#mongodb-parameter-param.ttlMonitorEnabled.
- CI publication waits for secret scanning as well as tests; the existing Build and tests check name remains stable after renaming its YAML job id to tests. PRs/manual dispatch do not publish; normal GitHub skip-ci behavior remains.

### Questions / catalog proposals
None. No parameter, event, notification, error code or catalog entry was added or changed. The first published-image/registry visibility and multi-architecture check remains the organizer's task-defined post-publication verification, not an executor access blocker.
